stands for the representative element of each conjugacy class.

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{2}}=\mathbf{1_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{3_{s}}\,,\quad\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}=\mathbf{4}\,,\quad\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\otimes\mathbf{3}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\,,\quad\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\otimes\mathbf{3^{\prime}}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{3}=\mathbf{\widehat{6}}\,,

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\otimes\mathbf{4}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}}\,,\quad\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}=\mathbf{3}\oplus\mathbf{5}\,,\quad\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\otimes\mathbf{5}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{5}=\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}}\,,

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{6}}=\mathbf{3}\otimes\mathbf{4}=\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{4}\oplus\mathbf{5}\,,\quad\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}=\mathbf{1_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{s}}\,,\quad\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{3^{\prime}}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\,,

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{4}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}}\,,\quad\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}=\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{5}\,,\quad\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{6}}=\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{4}=\mathbf{3}\oplus\mathbf{4}\oplus\mathbf{5}\,,

\displaystyle\mathbf{3}\otimes\mathbf{3}=\mathbf{1_{s}}\oplus\mathbf{3_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{s}}\,,\quad\mathbf{3}\otimes\mathbf{3^{\prime}}=\mathbf{4}\oplus\mathbf{5}\,,\quad\mathbf{3}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}}\,,

\displaystyle\mathbf{3}\otimes\mathbf{5}=\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{5}=\mathbf{3}\oplus\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{4}\oplus\mathbf{5}\,,\quad\mathbf{3}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{6}}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{1}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{2}}\,,

\displaystyle\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{3^{\prime}}=\mathbf{1_{s}}\oplus\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{s}}\,,\quad\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}}\,,\quad\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{6}}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{1}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{2}}\,,

\displaystyle\mathbf{4}\otimes\mathbf{4}=\mathbf{1_{s}}\oplus\mathbf{3_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{4_{s}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{s}}\,,\quad\mathbf{4}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}=\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{1}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{2}}\,,

\displaystyle\mathbf{4}\otimes\mathbf{5}=\mathbf{3}\oplus\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{4}\oplus\mathbf{5_{1}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{2}}\,,\quad\mathbf{4}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{6}}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}_{1}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}_{2}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{1}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{2}}\,,

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}=\mathbf{1_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{3_{s}}\oplus\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{s}}\oplus\mathbf{4_{s}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{a}}\,,\quad\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{5}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{1}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{2}}\,,

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{6}}=\mathbf{3}\oplus\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{4_{1}}\oplus\mathbf{4_{2}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{1}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{2}}\,,

\displaystyle\mathbf{5}\otimes\mathbf{5}=\mathbf{1_{s}}\oplus\mathbf{3_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{4_{s}}\oplus\mathbf{4_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{1,s}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{2,s}}\,,

\displaystyle\mathbf{5}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{6}}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}_{1}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}_{2}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{1}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{2}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{3}}\,,

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{6}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{6}}=\mathbf{1_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{3_{1,s}}\oplus\mathbf{3_{2,s}}\oplus\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{1,s}}\oplus\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{2,s}}\oplus\mathbf{4_{s}}\oplus\mathbf{4_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{1,s}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{2,a}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{3,a}}\,,

(622)

where the subscripts
\mathbf{s}
and
\mathbf{a}
denote symmetric and antisymmetric combinations respectively. For the product decomposition
\mathbf{3}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{6}}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{1}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{2}}
,
\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{1}}
and
\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{2}}
refer to the two sextet representations appearing in the tensor products of
\mathbf{3}
and
\mathbf{\widehat{6}}
, and similar notations are adopted for other tensor products. We now list the Clebsch-Gordan coefficients which are quite useful in model construction. we use
\alpha_{i}
to indicate the elements of the first representation of the product and
\beta_{i}
to indicate those of the second representation.

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{2}}=\mathbf{1_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{3_{s}}

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{1_{a}}\sim\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{3_{s}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{4}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
-\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

(626)

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\otimes\mathbf{3}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\otimes\mathbf{3^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{6}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
-\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
-\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\\
-\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\\
-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

(630)

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\otimes\mathbf{4}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}}

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{\widehat{6}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
-\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}=\mathbf{3}\oplus\mathbf{5}

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{3}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{5}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
2\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}\\
2\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\otimes\mathbf{5}=\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}}

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}2\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-2\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{\widehat{6}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}-2\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-2\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
2\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
-2\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{6}}=\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{4}\oplus\mathbf{5}

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{4}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
-\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{5}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}\\
-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}-2\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}-2\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+2\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}-2\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}=\mathbf{1_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{s}}

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{1_{a}}\sim\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{s}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{6}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\\
-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

(647)

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{3^{\prime}}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{4}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}}

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{\widehat{6}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\\
-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}=\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{5}

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{5}\sim\begin{pmatrix}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
-2\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}\\
-2\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{5}=\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}}

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\\
-2\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}\\
2\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{\widehat{6}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}3\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
3\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}+2\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}\\
2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-2\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{6}}=\mathbf{3}\oplus\mathbf{4}\oplus\mathbf{5}

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{3}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{4}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}\\
-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{5}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{6}\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
3\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}\\
2\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}-2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}\\
2\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}-2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}-3\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\mathbf{3}\otimes\mathbf{3}=\mathbf{1_{s}}\oplus\mathbf{3_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{s}}

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{1_{s}}\sim\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{3_{a}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\\
\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{5_{s}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}2\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}\\
-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\mathbf{3}\otimes\mathbf{3^{\prime}}=\mathbf{4}\oplus\mathbf{5}

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{4}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}\\
-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}\\
-\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{5}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\mathbf{3}\otimes\mathbf{4}=\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{4}\oplus\mathbf{5}

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}\\
\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{4}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}\\
-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{5}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}\\
2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}+2\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}\\
3\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}-3\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}\\
-2\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\mathbf{3}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}}

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}-3\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}\\
-2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}-2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}+3\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{\widehat{6}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}\\
-2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}-2\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-2\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{3}\otimes\mathbf{5}=\mathbf{3}\oplus\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{4}\\
\qquad\oplus\mathbf{5}\end{array}\right.

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{3}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}-2\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}\\
\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{4}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}+2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\\
2\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}-3\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}\\
3\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}-2\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}-2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{5}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}\\
-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-2\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
2\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{3}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{6}}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{1}}\\
\qquad\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{2}}\end{array}\right.

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
-\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-2\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{6}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}\\
2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}-2\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{1}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{\widehat{6}_{2}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{6}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{3^{\prime}}=\mathbf{1_{s}}\oplus\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{s}}

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{1_{s}}\sim\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{a}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}\\
\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\\
\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{5_{s}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}2\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}\\
-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\\
-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\mathbf{3^{\prime}}\otimes\mathbf{4}=\mathbf{3}\oplus\mathbf{4}\oplus\mathbf{5}

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{3}\sim\begin{pmatrix}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}\\

-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{4}\beta_{5}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}+\alpha_{4}\beta_{6}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}\\
-\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{4_{2}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{4}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}\\
\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-2\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}-2\alpha_{3}\beta_{6}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{4}\beta_{6}+2\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}-2\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}\\
-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{4}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{5_{1}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{4}\beta_{4}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{6}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{4}\beta_{6}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{4}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{5_{2}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{6}\alpha_{4}\beta_{3}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}\\
2\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{4}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{4}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{6}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}+\alpha_{4}\beta_{6}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}-2\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{5}\otimes\mathbf{5}=\mathbf{1_{s}}\oplus\mathbf{3_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{a}}\\
\qquad\quad\oplus\mathbf{4_{s}}\oplus\mathbf{4_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{1,s}}\\
\qquad\oplus\mathbf{5_{2,s}}\end{array}\right.

with

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{1_{s}}\sim\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{5}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}+\alpha_{4}\beta_{3}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}\\[7.22743pt]

\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{5}\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]

\sqrt{3}\alpha_{4}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{4_{s}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}4\alpha_{4}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{5}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}\\

\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]

\sqrt{2}\alpha_{4}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{5}\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]

\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{5_{2,s}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}2\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{4}\beta_{3}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}-2\alpha_{5}\beta_{2}-2\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}\\

\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}-2\alpha_{5}\beta_{1}-2\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{5}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{6}}=\mathbf{\widehat{2}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}_{1}}\\

\sqrt{3}\alpha_{5}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{5}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}-2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{\widehat{6}}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{6}}=\mathbf{1_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{3_{1,s}}\oplus\mathbf{3_{2,s}}\\
\qquad\quad\oplus\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{1,s}}\oplus\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{2,s}}\oplus\mathbf{4_{s}}\\
\qquad\quad\oplus\mathbf{4_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{1,s}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{2,a}}\\
\qquad\quad\oplus\mathbf{5_{3,a}}\end{array}\right.

with

\displaystyle\small\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbf{1_{a}}\sim\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{6}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{6}+\alpha_{5}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{5}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{3_{1,s}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{6}\beta_{3}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{6}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{5}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{5}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{5}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{4}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{3_{2,s}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}2\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}+2\alpha_{5}\beta_{4}+2\alpha_{4}\beta_{5}-2\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-2\alpha_{6}\beta_{4}-2\alpha_{4}\beta_{6}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}-2\alpha_{5}\beta_{3}-2\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{1,s}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{6}\beta_{3}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{6}\\
\alpha_{6}\beta_{5}+\alpha_{5}\beta_{6}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}\\
-\alpha_{5}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{2,s}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{6}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{6}-\alpha_{5}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{5}\\
\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{6}+\alpha_{5}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}+\alpha_{5}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{4_{s}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}+2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}+\alpha_{6}\beta_{4}+\alpha_{4}\beta_{6}-2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{5}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}-3\alpha_{4}\beta_{1}-3\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{6}\beta_{5}-\alpha_{5}\beta_{6}\\
2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{6}+\alpha_{5}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}-3\alpha_{5}\beta_{2}-3\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}+2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{2}+2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}-2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{4}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{5}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{4_{a}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{4}\beta_{6}-\alpha_{6}\beta_{4}\\
\alpha_{4}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{6}\beta_{5}-\alpha_{5}\beta_{6}\\
\alpha_{5}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}+\alpha_{5}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}-\alpha_{5}\beta_{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{5_{1,s}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\

2\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{5}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{5}\beta_{6}\\
2\alpha_{6}\beta_{6}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{4}\beta_{3}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{5}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}\\

\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{5_{2,a}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+2\alpha_{5}\beta_{4}-2\alpha_{4}\beta_{5}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{6}-\alpha_{6}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{6}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{5}\beta_{6}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{4}\beta_{3}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{5}\beta_{1}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{6}\beta_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\[7.22743pt]
\mathbf{5_{3,a}}\sim\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{5}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{4}\beta_{5}+2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{6}-2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{6}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{4}\beta_{6}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{4}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{5}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{5}\beta_{1}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{6}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{5}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{6}\beta_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}\end{array}\right.

C.4.1
Integer weight modular forms of level 5

The linear space of modular forms of positive integer weight
k
and level 5 has dimension
5k+1
. A general vector
\mathcal{M}_{k}(\Gamma(5))
in the linear space can be explicitly constructed through the Dedekind eta-function and Klein form as follow
schultz2015notes
;
Yao:2020zml
:

\mathcal{M}_{k}(\Gamma(5))=\sum_{m+n=5k,\,m,n\geq 0}c_{mn}\frac{\eta^{15k}(5\tau)}{\eta^{3k}(\tau)}\mathfrak{k}^{m}_{\frac{1}{5},0}(5\tau)\mathfrak{k}^{n}_{\frac{2}{5},0}(5\tau)\,,

(810)

where the Dedekind eta-function
\eta(\tau)
is defined in Eq. (
118
) and Klein form
\mathfrak{k}_{(r_{1},r_{2})}(\tau)
is given by
K_lang1981
;
lang1987elliptic
;
lang2012introduction
;
eum2011modularity
:

\mathfrak{k}_{(r_{1},r_{2})}(\tau)=q^{(r_{1}-1)/2}_{z}(1-q_{z})\prod_{n=1}^{\infty}(1-q^{n}q_{z})(1-q^{n}q_{z}^{-1})(1-q^{n})^{-2}\,,

(811)

with
q_{z}=e^{2\pi iz}
and
z=r_{1}\tau+r_{2}
. From Eq. (
810
), we known that the modular space of level 5 can be generated by
F_{1}(\tau)
and
F_{2}(\tau)
,

F_{1}(\tau)=\dfrac{\eta^{3}(5\tau)\mathfrak{k}_{\frac{2}{5},0}(5\tau)}{\eta(\tau)^{3/5}},~~~F_{2}(\tau)=\dfrac{\eta^{3}(5\tau)\mathfrak{k}_{\frac{1}{5},0}(5\tau)}{\eta(\tau)^{3/5}}\,,

(812)

which turn out to be weight
1/5
modular forms of level 5
ibukiyama2000modular
;
Yao:2020zml
. Each modular form of integer weight
k
and level 5 can be written as a polynomial of degree
5k
in
F_{1}(\tau)
and
F_{2}(\tau)
:

\sum_{n=0}^{5k}c_{n}F^{n}_{1}(\tau)F^{5k-n}_{2}(\tau)\,.

(813)

Because
F_{1}(\tau)
and
F_{2}(\tau)
are algebraically independent, all terms in above polynomial are linearly independent, and obviously the number of independent terms matches with the correct dimension
5k+1
. Without loss of generality, we can choose a set of basis vectors of the weight 1 modular space as

F^{5}_{1}(\tau),~~F^{4}_{1}(\tau)F_{2}(\tau),~~F^{3}_{1}(\tau)F^{2}_{2}(\tau),~~F^{2}_{1}(\tau)F^{3}_{2}(\tau),~~F_{1}(\tau)F^{4}_{2}(\tau),~~F^{5}_{2}(\tau)\,.

(814)

In the representation basis of Eq. (
C.4
), the above six modular forms can be organized into a six dimensional representation
\mathbf{\widehat{6}}
of
\Gamma^{\prime}_{5}=A^{\prime}_{5}
:

Y^{(1)}_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}(\tau)=\begin{pmatrix}F_{1}^{5}+2F_{2}^{5}\\
2F_{1}^{5}-F_{2}^{5}\\
5F_{1}^{4}F_{2}\\
5\sqrt{2}F_{1}^{3}F_{2}^{2}\\
-5\sqrt{2}F_{1}^{2}F_{2}^{3}\\
5F_{1}F_{2}^{4}\end{pmatrix}\equiv\begin{pmatrix}Y_{1}(\tau)\\
Y_{2}(\tau)\\
Y_{3}(\tau)\\
Y_{4}(\tau)\\
Y_{5}(\tau)\\
Y_{6}(\tau)\end{pmatrix}\,,

(815)

which fulfills the identities in Eqs. (
112
,
113
). The
q
-expansion of the modular forms
Y_{1,2,3,4,5,6}
reads as

\displaystyle Y_{1}(\tau)=1+5q+10q^{3}-5q^{4}+5q^{5}+10q^{6}+5q^{9}+\ldots\,,

\displaystyle Y_{2}(\tau)=2+5q+10q^{2}+5q^{4}+5q^{5}+10q^{6}+10q^{7}-5q^{9}+\ldots\,,

\displaystyle Y_{3}(\tau)=5q^{1/5}\left(1+2q+2q^{2}+q^{3}+2q^{4}+2q^{5}+2q^{6}+q^{7}+2q^{8}+2q^{9}+\ldots\right)\,,

\displaystyle Y_{4}(\tau)=5\sqrt{2}q^{2/5}\left(1+q+q^{2}+q^{3}+2q^{4}+q^{6}+q^{7}+2q^{8}+q^{9}+\ldots\right)\,,

\displaystyle Y_{5}(\tau)=-5\sqrt{2}q^{3/5}\left(1+q^{2}+q^{3}+q^{4}-q^{5}+2q^{6}+q^{8}+q^{9}+\ldots\right)\,,

\displaystyle Y_{6}(\tau)=5q^{4/5}\left(1-q+2q^{2}+2q^{6}-2q^{7}+2q^{8}+q^{9}+\ldots\right)\,.

(816)

The higher weight modular forms can be constructed from tensor products of
Y^{(1)}_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}(\tau)
. For example, there are 11 independent weight 2 modular forms of level 5, which can be decomposed into two triplets and a quintet transforming in the
\mathbf{3}
,
\mathbf{3^{\prime}}
and
\mathbf{5}
irreducible representations of
A^{\prime}_{5}
. Without loss of generality we can choose the weight 2 and level 5 modular forms as

\begin{split}Y_{\mathbf{3}}^{(2)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)})_{\mathbf{3}_{1,s}}=\begin{pmatrix}-2\left(Y_{1}Y_{2}+Y_{4}Y_{5}-Y_{3}Y_{6}\right)\\
\sqrt{2}\left(Y_{5}^{2}-2Y_{2}Y_{3}\right)\\
-\sqrt{2}\left(Y_{4}^{2}+2Y_{1}Y_{6}\right)\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{3^{\prime}}}^{(2)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)})_{\mathbf{3^{\prime}}_{1,s}}=\begin{pmatrix}2\left(Y_{1}Y_{2}+Y_{3}Y_{6}\right)\\
2Y_{5}Y_{6}-2Y_{2}Y_{4}\\
-2\left(Y_{3}Y_{4}+Y_{1}Y_{5}\right)\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{5}}^{(2)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)})_{\mathbf{5}_{1,s}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\sqrt{6}\left(Y_{1}^{2}+Y_{2}^{2}\right)\\
2\left(Y_{5}^{2}+Y_{1}Y_{3}+Y_{2}Y_{3}+\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{6}\right)\\
2\left(Y_{3}^{2}+\sqrt{2}\left(Y_{2}Y_{4}+Y_{5}Y_{6}\right)\right)\\
2\left(Y_{6}^{2}+\sqrt{2}Y_{3}Y_{4}-\sqrt{2}Y_{1}Y_{5}\right)\\
2\left(Y_{4}^{2}-\sqrt{2}Y_{3}Y_{5}+\left(Y_{2}-Y_{1}\right)Y_{6}\right)\\
\end{pmatrix}\,.\end{split}

(817)

The linear space of modular forms of weight
k=3
and level 5 has dimension
5k+1=16
, and they can be decomposed into a quartet and two sextets transforming as
\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}
and
\mathbf{\widehat{6}}
under
A^{\prime}_{5}
,

\begin{split}Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}}^{(3)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{3^{\prime}}}^{(2)})_{\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\sqrt{6}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},1}^{(2)}-\sqrt{3}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},2}^{(2)}+\sqrt{6}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},3}^{(2)}\\
-2Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},1}^{(2)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},2}^{(2)}-3Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},2}^{(2)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},3}^{(2)}\\
-2Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},1}^{(2)}-Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},2}^{(2)}+\left(3Y_{1}+Y_{2}\right)Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},3}^{(2)}\\
-\sqrt{6}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},1}^{(2)}-\sqrt{6}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},2}^{(2)}+\sqrt{3}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},3}^{(2)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}I}^{(3)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{3}}^{(2)})_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}_{1}}=\begin{pmatrix}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{3},1}^{(2)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{3},3}^{(2)}\\
Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{3},1}^{(2)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{3},2}^{(2)}\\
Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{3},1}^{(2)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{3},2}^{(2)}\\
\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{3},3}^{(2)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{3},1}^{(2)}\\
Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{3},1}^{(2)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{3},2}^{(2)}\\
\sqrt{2}Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{3},3}^{(2)}-Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{3},1}^{(2)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}II}^{(3)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{5}}^{(2)})_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}_{3}}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{2}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{5},1}^{(2)}+\sqrt{3}\left(Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5},2}^{(2)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5},3}^{(2)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5},4}^{(2)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5},5}^{(2)}\right)\\
\sqrt{2}Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{5},1}^{(2)}+\sqrt{3}\left(Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5},2}^{(2)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5},3}^{(2)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5},4}^{(2)}-Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5},5}^{(2)}\right)\\
\sqrt{3}\left(Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{5},2}^{(2)}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{5},2}^{(2)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5},5}^{(2)}\right)-2\sqrt{2}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5},1}^{(2)}\\
\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5},1}^{(2)}+\sqrt{6}\left(Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5},2}^{(2)}+\left(Y_{2}-Y_{1}\right)Y_{\mathbf{5},3}^{(2)}\right)\\
\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5},1}^{(2)}-\sqrt{6}\left(Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{5},4}^{(2)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{5},4}^{(2)}-Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5},5}^{(2)}\right)\\
\sqrt{3}\left(\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5},2}^{(2)}+\left(Y_{1}+Y_{2}\right)Y_{\mathbf{5},5}^{(2)}\right)-2\sqrt{2}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5},1}^{(2)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,\end{split}

(818)

where we denote the notation
Y_{\mathbf{3}}^{(2)}\equiv(Y_{\mathbf{3},1}^{(2)},Y_{\mathbf{3},2}^{(2)},Y_{\mathbf{3},3}^{(2)})^{T}
,
Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime}}^{(2)}\equiv(Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},1}^{(2)},Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},2}^{(2)},Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},3}^{(2)})^{T}
and
Y_{\mathbf{5}}^{(2)}\equiv(Y_{\mathbf{5},1}^{(2)},Y_{\mathbf{5},2}^{(2)},Y_{\mathbf{5},3}^{(2)},Y_{\mathbf{5},4}^{(2)},Y_{\mathbf{5},5}^{(2)})^{T}
, and similar notations are adopted for the modular forms in the following.

The weight 4 modular multiplets can be generated from the tensor product of
Y^{(1)}_{\mathbf{6}}
and the modular forms of weight 3. We find there are 21 linearly independent modular forms which can be chosen to be

\begin{split}Y_{\mathbf{1}}^{(4)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}I}^{(3)})_{\mathbf{1}_{a}}=Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,1}^{(3)}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,2}^{(3)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,3}^{(3)}-Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,6}^{(3)}+Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,4}^{(3)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,5}^{(3)}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{3}}^{(4)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}II}^{(3)})_{\mathbf{3}_{1,s}}=\begin{pmatrix}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,2}^{(3)}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,1}^{(3)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,6}^{(3)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,5}^{(3)}-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,4}^{(3)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,3}^{(3)}\\
-\sqrt{2}Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,3}^{(3)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,2}^{(3)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,5}^{(3)}\\
-\sqrt{2}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,6}^{(3)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,4}^{(3)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,1}^{(3)}\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{3^{\prime}}}^{(4)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}I}^{(3)})_{\mathbf{3^{\prime}}_{2,s}}=\begin{pmatrix}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}-Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}-Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\\
Y_{4}\left(Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}-Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}\right)-\sqrt{2}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+\left(Y_{2}-Y_{1}\right)Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}\\
Y_{5}\left(Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}+Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}\right)+(Y_{1}+Y_{2})Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{4}}^{(4)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}I}^{(3)})_{\mathbf{4}_{a}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\sqrt{2}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}-Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}+Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\\
Y_{4}\left(Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}-Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}\right)-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\\
Y_{5}\left(Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}+Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}\right)-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}\\
\sqrt{2}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{5}I}^{(4)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}I}^{(3)})_{\mathbf{5}_{1,s}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\sqrt{6}\left(Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}\right)\\
Y_{3}\left(Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}+Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}\right)+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}+2Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\\
\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}+2Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+\sqrt{2}\left(Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}+Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\right)\\
-\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}+2Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\\
Y_{6}\left(Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}-Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}\right)-\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+2Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{5}II}^{(4)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}I}^{(3)})_{\mathbf{5}_{2,a}}=\begin{pmatrix}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}-Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+2Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}-2Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\\

\sqrt{3}\left(-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}\right)\\
\sqrt{6}\left(Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}-Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}\right)\\
\end{pmatrix}\,.\end{split}

Notice that each entry of these weight four modular forms can be expressed as quartic polynomial of
Y_{1,2,3,4,5,6}
. In a same manner, we can obtain the weight 5 modular multiplets as follow,

\begin{split}Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{2}}}^{(5)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{5}II}^{(4)})_{\mathbf{2}}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{3}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,1}^{(4)}-2Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,2}^{(4)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}+2Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}\\

\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}}^{(5)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{5}II}^{(4)})_{\mathbf{2^{\prime}}}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{6}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,1}^{(4)}+3Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,2}^{(4)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,2}^{(4)}-2Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}+2\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}\\
\sqrt{6}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,1}^{(4)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,2}^{(4)}-2\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}+2Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}+3Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}}^{(5)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{5}II}^{(4)})_{\mathbf{4^{\prime}}_{1}}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{6}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,1}^{(4)}-2Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,2}^{(4)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}\\
\sqrt{3}\left(Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}-Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}\right)\\
\sqrt{3}\left(\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,2}^{(4)}-Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}+\left(Y_{1}-Y_{2}\right)Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}\right)\\
-\sqrt{6}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,1}^{(4)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,2}^{(4)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}-2Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}I}^{(5)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{1}}^{(4)})_{\mathbf{6}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{1},1}^{(4)}\\

Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}III}^{(5)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{5}II}^{(4)})_{\mathbf{6}_{2}}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{3}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,1}^{(4)}-2Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}\\

In the end, we give the linearly independent weight 6 modular multiplets of level 5,

\displaystyle Y_{\mathbf{1}}^{(6)}

\displaystyle=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}II}^{(5)})_{\mathbf{1}_{a}}=Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,1}^{(5)}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,2}^{(5)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,3}^{(5)}+Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,4}^{(5)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,5}^{(5)}-Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,6}^{(5)}\,,

\displaystyle Y_{\mathbf{3^{\prime}}II}^{(6)}

\displaystyle Y_{\mathbf{4}I}^{(6)}

\displaystyle=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{2}^{\prime}}}^{(5)})_{\mathbf{4}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{2}^{\prime},2}^{(5)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{2}^{\prime},1}^{(5)}\\

\displaystyle Y_{\mathbf{5}I}^{(6)}

\displaystyle=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{4}^{\prime}}}^{(5)})_{\mathbf{5}_{1}}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{6}\left(Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},2}^{(5)}+Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},3}^{(5)}\right)\\
\sqrt{6}Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},1}^{(5)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},2}^{(5)}+Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},3}^{(5)}-\sqrt{3}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},4}^{(5)}\\
\sqrt{2}\left(-\sqrt{3}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},1}^{(5)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},2}^{(5)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},2}^{(5)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},3}^{(5)}\right)\\
\sqrt{2}\left(-Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},2}^{(5)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},3}^{(5)}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},3}^{(5)}+\sqrt{3}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},4}^{(5)}\right)\\
-\sqrt{3}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},1}^{(5)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},2}^{(5)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},3}^{(5)}+\sqrt{6}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},4}^{(5)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

\displaystyle Y_{\mathbf{5}II}^{(6)}

\displaystyle=(Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{\widehat{6}}II}^{(5)})_{\mathbf{5}_{2,a}}=\begin{pmatrix}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,1}^{(5)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,2}^{(5)}-Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,3}^{(5)}+2Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,4}^{(5)}-2Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,5}^{(5)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,6}^{(5)}\\
\sqrt{6}\left(Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,3}^{(5)}-Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,1}^{(5)}\right)\\
\sqrt{3}\left(Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,2}^{(5)}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,4}^{(5)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,5}^{(5)}-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,6}^{(5)}\right)\\
\sqrt{3}\left(-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,1}^{(5)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,3}^{(5)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,4}^{(5)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,5}^{(5)}\right)\\
\sqrt{6}\left(Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,6}^{(5)}-Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,2}^{(5)}\right)\end{pmatrix}\,.

We see that the linear space of modular forms of level 5 and weight 6 has dimension 31 which is in agreement with the dimension formula
5k+1=5\times 6+1=31
. The above modular multiplets and their representations under
A^{\prime}_{5}
are listed in table
22
.

For higher level
N=6
and
N=7
, the interested readers can refer to Refs.
Li:2021buv
;
Ding:2020msi
for the details of relevant group theory and modular forms.
